# 2. Security rule for newly installed credentials

Never print, cat, log, commit, or include the actual token values in a handoff.

Safe verification may check only:

```text
variable exists
variable non-empty
container receives variable
API request succeeds
credential scope is adequate
```

Never include:

```text
token value
Authorization header
.env file contents
shell history containing secret
```

If a token is malformed or missing, report the variable name and failure only.

# 3. Phase A — activate Jira live and prove end-to-end behavior

The Jira integration was previously complete in mock mode. The human has now completed the token setup.

## A1. Verify configuration without exposing secrets

Confirm CT122 has:

```text
ACMS_JIRA_BASE_URL
ACMS_JIRA_EMAIL
ACMS_JIRA_API_TOKEN
```

Expected identity:

```text
startupteamscompany@gmail.com
```

Expected site:

```text
https://startupteams.atlassian.net
```

If project allow-list is empty, treat that as:

```text
all projects visible to the account
```

unless current code semantics differ.

Recreate/restart only the required ACMS service and pin the current production image tag.

Do not repeat the prior mistake of running `docker compose up -d` without `ACMS_APP_IMAGE_TAG`.

## A2. Read-only authentication proof

Call the Jira APIs for:

```text
current account
visible projects
issues assigned to startupteamscompany@gmail.com
```

Record:

```text
auth success/failure
visible project keys
assigned issue count
```

Do not record secret material.

## A3. Discover workflow transitions

For every project that currently has AI-assigned work, inspect real Jira transitions.

Create/update a mapping such as:

```text
project_key:
  semantic_ready:
  semantic_in_progress:
  semantic_in_review:
  semantic_done:
```

Do not hard-code one Jira status name globally.

Rules:

```text
AI-assigned issue:
  READY/APPROVED → IN_PROGRESS
  IN_PROGRESS → IN_REVIEW

AI does NOT automatically move:
  IN_REVIEW → DONE/CLOSED/ACCEPTED
```

Humans retain final acceptance.

For issues assigned to someone other than the Startup Teams AI account:

```text
do not change status
```

## A4. One bounded live E2E proof

Choose one safe issue already assigned to `startupteamscompany@gmail.com`.

If no suitable issue exists, stop and report that no safe test issue exists rather than creating Jira noise unless issue creation is already explicitly authorized.

Prove:

```text
read Jira issue
→ create/correlate high-level ACMS context
→ move to semantic In Progress if appropriate
→ post one concise progress/comment
→ attach/link relevant PR or artifact if available
→ move to semantic In Review only when deliverable is genuinely ready
```

Do not move it to Done/Closed.

## A5. Comment format

Keep Jira comments deliberately short.

Software example:

```text
Implementation ready for review.
PR: https://github.com/startupteams/<repo>/pull/<n>
```

Blocked example:

```text
Blocked: <one sentence>
Needs: <one sentence>
```

Do not paste GitHub PR descriptions into Jira.

## A6. Noise invariant

Do not post Jira comments for:

```text
individual tool calls
every ACMS session
every retry
every token update
every audit event
```

# 5. Phase C — make ADR-0012 runtime-driven, not model-instruction-dependent

## Current observation

Window 3 proved:

```text
ACMS callback endpoint works
reconciliation works
zero-manual-close works
```

but the GLM-fast Hermes session ignored the in-prompt completion protocol.

Therefore the accepted architecture is not yet fully realized:

```text
desired:
  runtime/Bridge callback = primary
  reconcile = fallback

current practical behavior:
  reconcile = primary when model ignores callback instruction
```

This must be fixed below the model reasoning layer.

## C1. Put callback in the runtime/Bridge lifecycle

Do not depend on the LLM deciding to call the completion endpoint.

The Agent Bridge / harness adapter should observe authoritative terminal task state:

```text
SUCCEEDED
FAILED
CANCELLED
```

and perform the callback automatically.

The callback is infrastructure behavior, not model behavior.

## C2. Callback payload

Use the accepted ADR-0012 contract:

```text
task_id
acms_agent_id
runtime_id if contract supports it
a2a_run_id
status
completed_at
result/handoff reference
usage/cost references if available
concise error summary on failure
```

No transcript or chain-of-thought.

## C3. Retry policy

Callback delivery must tolerate transient failure.

Implement bounded retry/backoff.

Example direction:

```text
immediate
10s
30s
60s
then rely on reconciliation
```

Use existing retry conventions if available rather than inventing a second retry framework.

## C4. Idempotency

Prove:

```text
callback delivered twice → one terminal transition
callback after reconcile → already_terminal / harmless
reconcile after callback → no duplicate close
network outage → callback retries then reconcile eventually repairs
```

## C5. Live proof

Run one bounded VM124 task.

Success requires:

```text
Hermes run reaches terminal
Bridge/runtime hook sends callback
ACMS receives callback BEFORE reconcile is required
task closes
ExecutionSession closes
no manual close
reconcile finds nothing to repair
```

Record the callback event and timing without secrets.

Checkpoint.

# 6. Phase D — eliminate AI direct-to-main bypass in practice

## Problem

Window 3 recorded two direct-to-main pushes even though PR-first policy was approved.

Root cause:

```text
AI agent is operating with an identity that has human-admin bypass authority
```

`enforce_admins=false` is intentional for humans, but the AI must not casually inherit that bypass.

This is a governance/security gap.

## D1. Immediate local fail-safe

Add a repository-local or agent-environment guard that refuses AI-initiated push to protected production branches.

Preferred implementation:

```text
central AI git wrapper or pre-push hook
```

that rejects:

```text
refs/heads/main
master
other configured protected production branches
```

unless an explicit emergency-human override is present.

The guard must not interfere with GitHub PR merge operations performed server-side.

## D2. Post-merge branch handling

After:

```text
gh pr merge --delete-branch
```

future agents must verify the local branch before any commit/push.

Automate this where possible rather than relying only on prose.

## D3. Long-term identity separation spike

Investigate a dedicated non-admin AI GitHub identity:

```text
GitHub App
machine user
other least-privilege automation identity
```

Requirements:

```text
can create branches/PRs
can merge PRs after checks if policy permits
cannot bypass protected main
cannot administer repository settings
```

If creating the identity requires human web UI action, create a short setup file and do not block this sprint.

## D4. Do not remove human admin emergency capability

Humans must retain intentional emergency/direct-main authority.

The goal is:

```text
human admin can bypass
AI agent cannot accidentally bypass
```

Checkpoint.

# 7. Phase E — harden LLM Manager release dependency reproducibility

## Current debt

Window 3 found:

```text
SQLAlchemy was in the freeze file
but missing from the VM114 production venv
manual pip install was required
the release transaction does not rebuild/verify the venv
```

This is a deployment reproducibility defect.

## E1. Dependency source of truth

Identify the canonical dependency input:

```text
requirements file / lock file / pyproject
```

Do not treat a manually modified production venv as authoritative.

## E2. Release preflight

Before service restart, the release pipeline should verify required runtime dependencies.

Preferred:

```text
install/sync dependencies from locked source
or verify exact versions and fail before cutover
```

Do not silently continue with missing dependencies.

## E3. Rollback compatibility

A release rollback must restore application code without leaving an incompatible dependency environment.

Document the dependency compatibility strategy.

## E4. Test

Prove on staging or a disposable venv:

```text
clean environment
→ release install
→ required imports
→ service startup
```

Then deploy through normal release pipeline.

Checkpoint.

# 15. PR / release discipline

Every code change:

```text
feature/fix branch
→ tests
→ PR
→ required checks
→ automated/self review
→ merge when green
→ release transaction
→ smoke/health
→ checkpoint
```

Do not use direct-to-main push as a normal shortcut.

If local tooling leaves the checkout on `main` after a merge, switch/create a branch before the next commit.

If a manual hop stage dies on a bare\n  name AFTER copying the maintenance conf, the host\n  `deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` is already switched —\n  `git checkout -- deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` BEFORE retrying, or the\n  retry double-applies maintenance content.\n- No `pg_dump`/`psql` on the CT host — run inside the postgres container:\n  `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yaml --env-file /opt/acms/.env exec -T\n  postgres pg_dump -U acms -Fc acms`.\n- No `curl` in the app image — probe with `docker exec acms-acms-app-1\n  python -c \"import urllib.request; ...\"` on 127.0.0.1:8000.\n- nginx allowlists 10.0.10.0/24 + 10.0.20.0/24 — validate via the VM's own IP\n  `https://10.0.20.122` with `curl -k` (self-signed; MARION has NO home.arpa\n  DNS — raw IPs everywhere).\n- `/version` returns `{version, git_sha, git_sha_short, build_time}` — the\n  git_sha identity check catches wrong-image states instantly. Run it after\n  ANY container recreation.\n- LLDAP 10.0.20.101:3890, base dc=example,dc=com; groups acms-admin/16\n  (jordatech), acms-workers/17, acms-observers/18.\n\n## Docker/Compose pitfalls (from the live drill — see references file)\n\n- **Single-file bind mounts pin the inode**: swapping the file on disk is\n  invisible to the container; HUP reload then serves stale config forever.\n  nginx now uses a directory mount (`./reverse-proxy:/etc/nginx/conf.d`) and\n  the maintenance template is named `*.conf.template` so the include glob\n  never loads it. Never re-introduce a single-file conf mount.\n- **`docker compose up -d <svc>` recreates depends_on services too** and can\n  bring the app back with the default image (`acms-app:local`). Pin\n  `ACMS_APP_IMAGE_TAG=<tag>` on EVERY compose up in tooling.\n- **Never command-substitute `compose build`** — its stdout is the build log\n  and pollutes captured variables (`invalid tag`). Send build output to\n  `>&2`; compute the tag, never capture it.\n- **pipefail + `grep -c` false positive**: `grep -c` exits 1 when the count\n  is 0; capture the count into a variable with `|| true`, compare numerically.\n- Recovery from stuck maintenance mode: `up -d --force-recreate\n  reverse-proxy`, then re-pin the app image, then verify via `/version`.\n\n## Bootstrap hop (chicken-and-egg first deploy)\n\nThe first deployment of the release tooling cannot use the tooling. Manual\nhop with identical guarantees: verified backup → stop app → checkout target\nSHA → build SHA-tagged image → migrate → start → validate → write release\nmetadata (mark `mode=manual-bootstrap`). Proven 2026-09-26 (~2 min window).\n\n## Verification hygiene (file-tool corruption lesson)\n\nGround truth for any file change is `git diff` / `py_compile` / `bash -n` —\nnever the tool result echo. Echoes can mislead in BOTH directions (phantom\nerrors and phantom success; this session had mid-write corruption injecting\ngarbage text). For large/critical writes: verify immediately after writing,\nand if corrupted, repair via a python rewrite with assertion pre-checks on\nknown anchor lines, then re-verify with git diff.\n\n## PR-discipline trap (hit TWICE on 2026-09-29 — mandatory habit)\n\n`gh pr merge --squash --delete-branch` checks the LOCAL checkout out onto\n`main` when it deletes the branch. A following `git push -q` (or any commit +\npush) then goes DIRECT to main — Jordan's token has admin rights, so branch\nprotection silently bypasses (remote prints \"Bypassed rule violations\" but the\npush lands). Happened twice in one window (c2d68d7, 8575982; content was\ngreen, the process was violated). **Habit: after every merge-with-delete-branch,\nrun `git branch --show-current` BEFORE any commit; if on main, `git switch -c\n<branch> origin/main` first.** Never `git push` bare from a merge aftermath.\n\n## ADR-0012 completion callback (PRs #45/#46, 2026-09-29, live)\n\n- `POST /api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion` — dedicated scoped token\n  `ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN` (never the admin token; unset ⇒ 503 fail-closed).\n  CT122 env has CALLBACK_TOKEN (hex) + `ACMS_CALLBACK_BASE_URL=https://10.0.20.122`\n  in /opt/acms/.env; compose passthroughs merged (PR #47, which also added\n  ACMS_JIRA_* + ACMS_GITHUB_ECONOMICS_TOKEN passthroughs).\n- Identity binding: task must belong to the claimed agent (mismatch → 403 +\n  durable EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED audit; unknown task → 404 + audit).\n  Duplicate/late → idempotent `already_terminal`. Success → no Attention item.\n- **dispatch_service now PRE-creates the execution task before the bridge\n  call**; the task id travels in the instruction (COMPLETION PROTOCOL block,\n  gated on ACMS_CALLBACK_BASE_URL); `external_task_id` = the REAL A2A run id.\n  The dispatch idempotency ledger MOVED to the EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event\n  metadata (dedupe_key → task_id) — code querying `external_task_id ==\n  \"disp-<key>\"` is stale.\n- **Live proof pattern (ACMS-WORK-000004):** dispatch → session auto-open →\n  Hermes run completes (LiteLLM SpendLogs row = cost-of-record) → worker GLM\n  IGNORED the in-instruction completion protocol → the telemetry-scheduler\n  reconcile sweep closed the task from bridge run status + the orphaned OPEN\n  session (both orderings: task-then-session and session-already-orphaned) →\n  zero manual close. The reconciler IS the working completion authority until\n  a runtime-side callback hook exists (Hermes api-server has no run-completion\n  webhook).\n- Live endpoint probes from the CT HOST work via curl; from INSIDE the app\n  container nginx 403s (container-network source not allowlisted) — same\n  rule as all CT122 API probing.\n\n## Pointers\n\n- `references/live-drill-2026-09-26.md` — bug-by-bug drill breakdown, exact\n  commands, recovery timeline.\n- `references/hermes-harness-live-tests-2026-09.md` — Hermes 0.17.0 live\n  control-mapping results (pause/resume unsupported, steer/interrupt verified),\n  scratch-profile setup recipe, bridge-to-ACMS wiring.\n- `references/hermes-worker-bringup.md` — the full worker-runtime bring-up recipe\n  (VM clone → Hermes venv install via qga → LLM gateway wiring incl. context caps →\n  api-server platform config shape → systemd unit → verification + traps) as\n  executed on acms-worker-001.\n- `references/ip-collision-10-0-20-203-case-2026-09-29.md` — the template-clone\n  impostor-IP case (symptoms, ARP diagnosis, VM108 renumber, generalization).\n- Repo docs: `deploy/README.md` (operator runbook), ADR-0009 + ADR-0010 (both\n  Accepted 2026-09-26/27), `docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md`, `SPRINT.md`\n  (current slices), handoffs under `docs/handoffs/`.\n\n## Test-suite hygiene (conftest patterns — keep using these)\n\n- **The shared SQLite unit DB persists rows across tests in one pytest run** —\n  any suite asserting event/row COUNTS must use the `clean_db` fixture\n  (conftest DELETEs all tables, FK-off during wipe). Symptom when missing:\n  count assertions see prior tests' rows (`assert 2 == 1`-style failures that\n  pass under `-x` in isolation but fail in full runs).\n- `get_settings()` is lru_cached — env-var fixtures must\n  `get_settings.cache_clear()` on entry AND exit (see\n  test_work_creation_guard.py pattern).\n- Endpoint tests: sync `TestClient(app)` + async dependency override\n  (`app.dependency_overrides[get_session] = async gen`), NOT\n  httpx.ASGITransport with a sync client (fails:\n  \"ASGITransport object has no attribute handle_request\").\n- Reconciler/scheduler tests monkeypatch `acms.bridge.get_bridge_for_agent`\n  (the attribute resolved at call time), not the scheduler module.\n- asyncio_mode=auto (pyproject) — plain `async def test_` works; `db` fixture\n  pattern: `async for session in get_session(): yield session`.\n\n## Human-credential playbook (learned 2026-09-29)\n\nWhen a plan authorizes an integration but the credential requires web-UI\ncreation (Atlassian API tokens, GitHub fine-grained PATs — NO REST create\npath exists for either):\n1. Search session archives (session_search across queries) + Honcho memory\n   for the credential BEFORE declaring it missing — but NEVER reuse unrelated\n   credentials found in context (wrong-boundary facts stay facts).\n2. Do NOT ask the human to re-paste passwords into chat (plan §1.2 pattern).\n3. Write an exact `HUMAN-SETUP-*.md` (step-by-step UI path + two install\n   options: human does it via ssh, or pastes into chat for staging into\n   /opt/acms/.env without echoing) + merge the compose passthrough so\n   activation = human adds env vars + container recreate.\n4. Continue all other work — never block the sprint on the credential.\n## Combined slice 3+4 state (deployed 2026-09-27, v0.6.0 @ 85f3c92)\n  policy); **ADR-0010 Accepted** — heartbeat 60s / STALE 300s / reconcile\n  3600s / fleet 86400s / alignment grace 120s / context warnings 70-85-95,\n  all config-backed via `ACMS_*` settings (settings.py gained the fields).\n- Human-readable keys `ACMS-WORK-000001` / `ACMS-ASG-000001` via the\n  `acms_key_counters` transactional counter table — never row counts, never\n  reused; allocated in `create_work_item` / `assign_primary`.\n- State dimensions stay separate (plan §5): connectivity is ACMS-derived\n  (UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE from contact recency), `agent_running`\n  is harness-reported tri-state — the UI shows both as independent facts.\n- Heartbeat = complete snapshot (`acms-heartbeat-v1`), unknown fields rejected\n  by the Pydantic model; semantic events only on material change (never\n  per-heartbeat spam); event sequences allocated via the same counter table.\n- Time-dependent tests: inject `now_fn` into TelemetryScheduler; watch for\n  tz-naive vs tz-aware datetimes when mixing `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` with\n  DB-loaded values (replace(tzinfo=utc) guard in the stale delta).\n- **Next steps logged in agents.md:** wire `ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS_JSON` into\n  CT122 env + dispatch/control buttons in the UI; slices 5–9 per master plan.\n\n## Recovery guardrails (PRs #30–#32, 2026-09-29, prod 6f772aa)\n\n- **PR #30 live bug (budget events FK):** `budget_service` wrote\n  `agent_id=\"\"` on BUDGET_SET/OVERRIDE/THRESHOLD_CROSSED →\n  `agent_events_agent_id_fkey` violation on prod PG; SQLite unit tests DON'T\n  enforce FKs so the whole suite passed while prod 500'd. Work-scoped events\n  now leave `agent_id` NULL. **Lesson: SQLite-with-FKs-off is a silent prod\n  gap class — when a unit DB is SQLite, grep for sentinel `\"\"` foreign-key\n  values before trusting green tests.** Candidate FUTURE_WORK: FK pragma in\n  the unit engine.\n- **C4 (PR #32):** `AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT` = informational, emitted\n  ONCE per state entry (scheduler in-memory dedupe dict, reset on state exit),\n  never per tick; severity `info`; NO auto-remediation (human direction:\n  never auto-stop). Attention/SSE allowlists both new event types.\n- **C5:** `acms/reassignment_guard.py` — max 3 Executive reassignments per\n  work item, counted from durable `REASSIGNMENT_RECORDED` audit events (no\n  second mutable counter table); identical retry (`changed_dimension=none`)\n  → `REASSIGNMENT_REFUSED` (does NOT consume budget — the refusal must not\n  use the same event type or the count is poisoned); 4th real attempt →\n  `REASSIGNMENT_LIMIT_REACHED` (Attention high) and auto-reassignment stops.\n  Every attempt must declare changed_dimension. The subjective \"<60%\n  instruction match\" rule is deliberately NOT automated.\n- **Real dispatch PROVEN 2026-09-29** (ACMS-WORK-000002 → VM124, budget-gated,\n  echo verified via bridge messages API, $0 local, 12,385/84 tokens in\n  LiteLLM SpendLogs; retry=duplicate; attention 0; SSE dispatched). Loop:\n  create work → assign worker → `PUT /api/v1/budgets/work/{id}` →\n  `POST /api/v1/dispatch/work/{id}` → verify via bridge →\n  `POST /api/v1/work/tasks/{id}/finish?new_status=SUCCEEDED` (only\n  SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELLED accepted) → close assignment → PATCH item to\n  completed. Bridge targets are IP-pinned in `ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS_JSON`\n  (`/opt/acms/.env`, compose passthrough exists); DNS indirection = future work.\n  **IP-collision trap:** see\n  `references/ip-collision-10-0-20-203-case-2026-09-29.md` — a template-clone\n  impostor (VM108) squatted on the worker's `.203` and bridge traffic hit the\n  wrong guest while every app-level check passed; diagnose at the ARP/MAC\n  layer before touching config.\n- **ACMS recovery policy counterpart:** ARM reconciler now max 5 attempts\n  (env `ARM_RECONCILE_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, prod unset → 5 active) with\n  failure_class/recovery_method/attempt audited per attempt and\n  `strategy_next: human_attention` at exhaustion — see the LLM Manager skill.\n\n## Agent-state ownership + architecture recs (PRs #33–#34, 2026-09-29, in prod 6f772aa)\n\n- **Work lifecycle default (PR #31, ADR-0011 Proposed — awaiting Jordan's\n  acceptance):** Work Item = ONE measurable goal; a goal may span multiple\n  execution sessions (crash/context-rotation/retry does NOT spawn a new Work\n  Item); the NEXT measurable goal = the next CHILD Work Item linked to the\n  same Feature/Program parent (smallest-safe parent/child representation;\n  Executive Agent may propose the next child only within the approved parent\n  scope, provenance recorded — no silent scope expansion)

Local: `bash deploy/build-release.sh . dist` → tar.gz + sha256.\n   Build the artifact from the MAIN TIP, not your feature branch: create a\n   throwaway `git switch -c tmp-deploy-<sha> origin/main`, build from there,\n   then switch back (2026-09-29 pattern; keeps the artifact == merged main).\n2. Serve: `python3 -m http.server 8899 --bind 0.0.0.0` from `dist/`\n   (Hermes background terminal); kill it after (`pkill -f 'http.server 8899'`).\n   **Simpler than http.server: `scp` the tarball+sha directly to\n   `vm114:/tmp/`** (VM114 has sshd via the dedicated key) — used successfully\n   2026-09-29 for both releases.\n3. On VM114: `sha256sum -c <tarball>.sha256` → OK. **The `.sha256` file must\n   be named `<tarball>.sha256` and sit next to it** — the build names the sha\n   after the CONTENT hash; rewrite the single line first\n   (`python -c` writing `<content-hash>  <tarball-name>`) or preflight aborts.\n   Also: **build-release.sh names tarballs with the SHORT (12-char) sha**\n   (`llm-manager-8b05afb5c586.tar.gz`) while GitHub artifact uploads use the\n   full `github.sha` — never match an artifact by full sha.\n4. Run the CURRENT deployment's `deploy-release.sh` (`/opt/llm-manager/current/deploy/deploy-release.sh <tarball>`)\n   via `setsid nohup ... > /tmp/deploy-<sha>.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &` (plain\n   nohup from a ssh 'bash -c' dies with the session; setsid survives).\n5. Poll `tail` of the log via ssh until `==> Release ACCEPTED (<full-sha>)`\n   + `healthcheck PASSED`. Note: `pgrep -f deploy-release` matches your OWN\n   ssh command line — filter with `pgrep -fl 'deploy-release.sh /tmp'` or\n   you'll read a phantom \"still running\".\n6. If `server_manager/` code changed: `systemctl restart server-manager-api`\n   (its own systemd unit; NOT restarted by the web-app transaction).\n7. Check `deploy/ ops/` diffs before releasing: if the transaction tooling\n   itself changed, do a same-tip drill first (tooling-mid-flight is unproven).\n8. Verify: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.108/healthz` reports the new git_sha.\n   Clean the tarball+sha out of `/tmp` after success.\n9. **Env persistence across releases:** systemd DROP-INS survive releases —\n   env set via drop-in files (e.g.\n   `/etc/systemd/system/llm-manager-web.service.d/orm-cutover.conf`) is NOT\n   lost when a new release ships; plain env vars passed at restart time ARE.\n   There is no `/opt/llm-manager/.env` (staging toggle learned this: `touch \"\"\n`).\n\n## Staging VM120 (10.0.20.131) — access reality\n\n- NO direct shell for the agent (no root ssh from workstation or VM114; the\n  CT130 self-hosted runner's `llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131` key is the ONLY\n  path, and that user's sudo is wrapper-only: bootstrap/deploy/healthcheck\n  wrappers — arbitrary sudo asks for a password).\n- Staging auto-deploys on main push via `deploy-staging.yml`. Fix + live\n    repair (re-aggregate 24h → 576 buckets) → VERIFY PASSED 0 failures.\n  - `sqlalchemy` was in the requirements freeze but NOT in the prod venv —\n    pip-installed 2.0.54 into `/opt/llm-manager/venv` (venv is NOT rebuilt by\n    the release transaction; freeze drift is now known debt).\n  - **The systemd unit's WorkingDirectory (/opt/llm-manager/app) is a STALE\n    static dir; the RUNNING process cwd is\n    `/opt/llm-manager/releases/<sha>/service/app`.** Scripts probing deployed\n    code must `sys.path.insert(0, \"/opt/llm-manager/current/app\")` — and\n    `current/app` is a symlink into the active release.\n  - ORM is NOT imported by the FastAPI app for these domains yet — the flag\n    gates the legacy call sites themselves; parity tests live in\n    tests/test_orm_slice5_cutover.py + tests/test_gpu_orm_slice6.py (pgserver;\n    set PG_HOST=<socket dir>, PG_USER=postgres, PG_PW=\"\" empty-string VALID —\n    `if pw is None` not `if not pw`, or empty trust passwords break).\n- Slice 4 done (PR #52): read-only reflective models for\n  `recovery_events / request_routing_log / electricity_rates / manager_settings`\n  (`server_manager/llm_manager/models/orm_slice4.py`), schema mapped from\n  prod via `information_schema` (verify live columns before writing a model —\n  dates are DATE not String, PKs vary). FROM \"LiteLLM_SpendLogs\"` needs\n  `-d litellm`, not `-d llmmanager`).\n- Parity tests (tests/server_manager/test_orm_parity.py, pgserver embedded):\n  - `schema.sql` carries pg_dump ≥17 ### [2026-09-29T17:51:24.846493+00:00] tool · tool: skill_view

{"success": true, "name": "github-pr-workflow", "description": "GitHub PR lifecycle: branch, commit, open, CI, merge.", "tags": ["GitHub", "Pull-Requests", "CI/CD", "Git", "Automation", "Merge"], "related_skills": ["github-auth", "github-code-review"], "content": "---\nname: github-pr-workflow\ndescription: \"GitHub PR lifecycle: branch, commit, open, CI, merge.\"\nversion: 1.1.0\nauthor: Hermes Agent\nlicense: MIT\nplatforms: [linux, macos, windows]\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    tags: [GitHub, Pull-Requests, CI/CD, Git, Automation, Merge]\n    related_skills: [github-auth, github-code-review]\n---\n\n# GitHub Pull Request Workflow\n\nComplete guide for managing the PR lifecycle. Each section shows the `gh` way first, then the `git` + `curl` fallback for machines without `gh`.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Authenticated with GitHub (see `github-auth` skill)\n- Inside a git repository with a GitHub remote\n\n### Quick Auth Detection\n\n```bash\n# Determine which method to use throughout this workflow\nif command -v gh &>/dev/null && gh auth status &>/dev/null; then\n  AUTH=\"gh\"\nelse\n  AUTH=\"git\"\n  # Ensure we have a token for API calls\n  if [ -z \"$GITHUB_TOKEN\" ]; then\n    if _hermes_env=\"${HERMES_HOME:-$HOME/.hermes}/.env\"; [ -f \"$_hermes_env\" ] && grep -q \"^GITHUB_TOKEN=\" \"$_hermes_env\"; then\n      GITHUB_TOKEN=$(grep \"^GITHUB_TOKEN=\" \"$_hermes_env\" | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 | tr -d '\\n\\r')\n    elif grep -q \"github.com\" ~/.git-credentials 2>/dev/null; then\n      GITHUB_TOKEN=$(grep \"github.com\" ~/.git-credentials 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's|https://[^:]*:\\([^@]*\\)@.*|\\1|')\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\necho \"Using: $AUTH\"\n```\n\n### Extracting Owner/Repo from the Git Remote\n\nMany `curl` commands need `owner/repo`. Extract it from the git remote:\n\n```bash\n# Works for both HTTPS and SSH remote URLs\nREMOTE_URL=$(git remote get-url origin)\nOWNER_REPO=$(echo \"$REMOTE_URL\" | sed -E 's|.*github\\.com[:/]||; s|\\.git$||')\nOWNER=$(echo \"$OWNER_REPO\" | cut -d/ -f1)\nREPO=$(echo \"$OWNER_REPO\" | cut -d/ -f2)\necho \"Owner: $OWNER, Repo: $REPO\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Branch Creation\n\nThis part is pure `git` — identical either way:\n\n```bash\n# Make sure you're up to date\ngit fetch origin\ngit checkout main && git pull origin main\n\n# Create and switch to a new branch\ngit checkout -b feat/add-user-authentication\n```\n\nBranch naming conventions:\n- `feat/description` — new features\n- `fix/description` — bug fixes\n- `refactor/description` — code restructuring\n- `docs/description` — documentation\n- `ci/description` — CI/CD changes\n\n## 2. Making Commits\n\nUse the agent's file tools (`write_file`, `patch`) to make changes, then commit:\n\n```bash\n# Stage specific files\ngit add src/auth.py src/models/user.py tests/test_auth.py\n\n# Commit with a conventional commit message\ngit commit -m \"feat: add JWT-based user authentication\n\n- Add login/register endpoints\n- Add User model with password hashing\n- Add auth middleware for protected routes\n- Add unit tests for auth flow\"\n```\n\nCommit message format (Conventional Commits):\n```\ntype(scope): short description\n\nLonger explanation if needed. Wrap at 72 characters.\n```\n\nTypes: `feat`, `fix`, `refactor`, `docs`, `test`, `ci`, `chore`, `perf`\n\n## 3. Pushing and Creating a PR\n\n### Push the Branch (same either way)\n\n```bash\ngit push -u origin HEAD\n```\n\n### Create the PR\n\n**With gh:**\n\n```bash\ngh pr create \\\n  --title \"feat: add JWT-based user authentication\" \\\n  --body \"## Summary\n- Adds login and register API endpoints\n- JWT token generation and validation\n\n## Test Plan\n- [ ] Unit tests pass\n\nCloses #42\"\n```\n\nOptions: `--draft`, `--reviewer user1,user2`, `--label \"enhancement\"`, `--base develop`\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n```bash\nBRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)\n\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  -H \"Accept: application/vnd.github.v3+json\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/pulls \\\n  -d \"{\n    \\\"title\\\": \\\"feat: add JWT-based user authentication\\\",\n    \\\"body\\\": \\\"## Summary\\nAdds login and register API endpoints.\\n\\nCloses #42\\\",\n    \\\"head\\\": \\\"$BRANCH\\\",\n    \\\"base\\\": \\\"main\\\"\n  }\"\n```\n\nThe response JSON includes the PR `number` — save it for later commands.\n\nTo create as a draft, add `\"draft\": true` to the JSON body.\n\n## 4. Monitoring CI Status\n\n### Check CI Status\n\n**With gh:**\n\n```bash\n# One-shot check\ngh pr checks\n\n# Watch until all checks finish (polls every 10s)\ngh pr checks --watch\n```\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n```bash\n# Get the latest commit SHA on the current branch\nSHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\n\n# Query the combined status\ncurl -s \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/commits/$SHA/status \\\n  | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\ndata = json.load(sys.stdin)\nprint(f\\\"Overall: {data['state']}\\\")\nfor s in data.get('statuses', []):\n    print(f\\\"  {s['context']}: {s['state']} - {s.get('description', '')}\\\")\"\n\n# Also check GitHub Actions check runs (separate endpoint)\ncurl -s \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/commits/$SHA/check-runs \\\n  | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\ndata = json.load(sys.stdin)\nfor cr in data.get('check_runs', []):\n    print(f\\\"  {cr['name']}: {cr['status']} / {cr['conclusion'] or 'pending'}\\\")\"\n```\n\n### Poll Until Complete (git + curl)\n\n```bash\n# Simple polling loop — check every 30 seconds, up to 10 minutes\nSHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\nfor i in $(seq 1 20); do\n  STATUS=$(curl -s \\\n    -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n    https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/commits/$SHA/status \\\n    | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['state'])\")\n  echo \"Check $i: $STATUS\"\n  if [ \"$STATUS\" = \"success\" ] || [ \"$STATUS\" = \"failure\" ] || [ \"$STATUS\" = \"error\" ]; then\n    break\n  fi\n  sleep 30\ndone\n```\n\n## 5. Auto-Fixing CI Failures\n\nWhen CI fails, diagnose and fix. This loop works with either auth method.\n\n### Step 1: Get Failure Details\n\n**With gh:**\n\n```bash\n# List recent workflow runs on this branch\ngh run list --branch $(git branch --show-current) --limit 5\n\n# View failed logs\ngh run view <RUN_ID> --log-failed\n```\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n```bash\nBRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)\n\n# List workflow runs on this branch\ncurl -s \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  \"https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/actions/runs?branch=$BRANCH&per_page=5\" \\\n  | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\nruns = json.load(sys.stdin)['workflow_runs']\nfor r in runs:\n    print(f\\\"Run {r['id']}: {r['name']} - {r['conclusion'] or r['status']}\\\")\"\n\n# Get failed job logs (download as zip, extract, read)\nRUN_ID=<run_id>\ncurl -s -L \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/actions/runs/$RUN_ID/logs \\\n  -o /tmp/ci-logs.zip\ncd /tmp && unzip -o ci-logs.zip -d ci-logs && cat ci-logs/*.txt\n```\n\n### Step 2: Fix and Push\n\nAfter identifying the issue, use file tools (`patch`, `write_file`) to fix it:\n\n```bash\ngit add <fixed_files>\ngit commit -m \"fix: resolve CI failure in <check_name>\"\ngit push\n```\n\n### Step 3: Verify\n\nRe-check CI status using the commands from Section 4 above.\n\n### Auto-Fix Loop Pattern\n\nWhen asked to auto-fix CI, follow this loop:\n\n1. Check CI status → identify failures\n2. Read failure logs → understand the error\n3. Use `read_file` + `patch`/`write_file` → fix the code\n4. `git add . && git commit -m \"fix: ...\" && git push`\n5. Wait for CI → re-check status\n6. Repeat if still failing (up to 3 attempts, then ask the user)\n\n## 6. Merging\n\n**With gh:**\n\n```bash\n# Squash merge + delete branch (cleanest for feature branches)\ngh pr merge --squash --delete-branch\n\n# Enable auto-merge (merges when all checks pass)\ngh pr merge --auto --squash --delete-branch\n```\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n```bash\nPR_NUMBER=<number>\n\n# Merge the PR via API (squash)\ncurl -s -X PUT \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/pulls/$PR_NUMBER/merge \\\n  -d \"{\n    \\\"merge_method\\\": \\\"squash\\\",\n    \\\"commit_title\\\": \\\"feat: add user authentication (#$PR_NUMBER)\\\"\n  }\"\n\n# Delete the remote branch after merge\nBRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)\ngit push origin --delete $BRANCH\n\n# Switch back to main locally\ngit checkout main && git pull origin main\ngit branch -d $BRANCH\n```\n\nMerge methods: `\"merge\"` (merge commit), `\"squash\"`, `\"rebase\"`\n\n### Enable Auto-Merge (curl)\n\n```bash\n# Auto-merge requires the repo to have it enabled in settings.\n# This uses the GraphQL API since REST doesn't support auto-merge.\nPR_NODE_ID=$(curl -s \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/pulls/$PR_NUMBER \\\n  | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['node_id'])\")\n\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/graphql \\\n  -d \"{\\\"query\\\": \\\"mutation { enablePullRequestAutoMerge(input: {pullRequestId: \\\\\\\"$PR_NODE_ID\\\\\\\", mergeMethod: SQUASH}) { clientMutationId } }\\\"}\"\n```\n\n## 7. Complete Workflow Example\n\n```bash\n# 1. Start from clean main\ngit checkout main && git pull origin main\n\n# 2. Branch\ngit checkout -b fix/login-redirect-bug\n\n# 3. (Agent makes code changes with file tools)\n\n# 4. Commit\ngit add src/auth/login.py tests/test_login.py\ngit commit -m \"fix: correct redirect URL after login\n\nPreserves the ?next= parameter instead of always redirecting to /dashboard.\"\n\n# 5. Push\ngit push -u origin HEAD\n\n# 6. Create PR (picks gh or curl based on what's available)\n# ... (see Section 3)\n\n# 7. Monitor CI (see Section 4)\n\n# 8. Merge when green (see Section 6)\n```\n\n## Useful PR Commands Reference\n\n| Action | gh | git + curl |\n|--------|-----|-----------|\n| List my PRs | `gh pr list --author @me` | `curl -s -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \"https://api.github.com/repos/$OWNER/$REPO/pulls?state=open\"` |\n| View PR diff | `gh pr diff` | `git diff main...HEAD` (local) or `curl -H \"Accept: application/vnd.github.diff\" ...` |\n| Add comment | `gh pr comment N --body \"...\"` | `curl -X POST .../issues/N/comments -d '{\"body\":\"...\"}'` |\n| Request review | `gh pr edit N --add-reviewer user` | `curl -X POST .../pulls/N/requested_reviewers -d '{\"reviewers\":[\"user\"]}'` |\n| Close PR | `gh pr close N` | `curl -X PATCH .../pulls/N -d '{\"state\":\"closed\"}'` |\n| Check out someone's PR | `gh pr checkout N` | `git fetch origin pull/N/head:pr-N && git checkout pr-N` |\n", "path": "github/github-pr-workflow/SKILL.md", "skill_dir": "/home/jordatech/.hermes/profiles/agent_stea004_entrepreneur/skills/github/github-pr-workflow", "linked_files": {"references": ["references/ci-troubleshooting.md", "references/conventional-commits.md"], "templates": ["templates/pr-body-bugfix.md", "templates/pr-body-feature.md"]}, "usage_hint": "To view linked files, call skill_view(name, file_path) where file_path is e.g. 'references/api.md' or 'assets/config.yaml'", "required_environment_variables": [], "required_commands": [], "missing_required_environment_variables": [], "missing_credential_files": [], "missing_required_commands": [], "setup_needed": false, "setup_skipped": false, "readiness_status": "available", "metadata": {"hermes": {"tags": ["GitHub", "Pull-Requests", "CI/CD", "Git", "Automation", "Merge"], "related_skills": ["github-auth", "github-code-review"]}}}

# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira↔ACMS sync service (v1, 2026-09-29 plan §6/Phase D).\n2|\n3|Inbound:  poll assigned Jira issues → high-level ACMS work context (one work\n4|          item per Jira issue MAX; Executive decomposes further internally).\n5|Outbound: useful lifecycle summaries posted as Jira comments (started /\n6|          milestone / blocked / PR available / completed / final handoff).\n7|NEVER:    one Jira ticket per internal Work Item or task (plan §D7 invariant).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from .jira_client import JiraClient, JiraError\n12|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService\n13|\n14|\n15|# Correlation: Jira key stored on the Work Item title/scope prefix.\n16|JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX = \"jira:\"\n17|\n18|\n19|def correlation_tag(issue_key: str) -> str:\n20|    return f\"{JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX}{issue_key}\"\n21|\n22|\n23|def extract_jira_key(scope_or_title: str) -> str | None:\n24|    idx = scope_or_title.find(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX)\n25|    if idx < 0:\n26|        return None\n27|    rest = scope_or_title[idx + len(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX):]\n28|    token = rest.split()[0].split(\"\\n\")[0].strip()\n29|    return token or None\n30|\n31|\n32|class JiraSyncService:\n33|    \"\"\"Stateless-ish orchestration over JiraClient; db session injected.\"\"\"\n34|\n35|    def __init__(self, client: JiraClient | None = None) -> None:\n36|        self.client = client or JiraClient()\n37|\n38|    async def inbound_assigned_issues(self, db) -> list[dict]:\n39|        \"\"\"Fetch assigned issues and record an inbound-sync event per issue.\n40|\n41|        Returns issue dicts for the Executive Agent to decompose. {"content": "1|\"\"\"Phase K — objective PR-metadata ingestion from GitHub (2026-09-29 plan §13).\n2|\n3|Ingests AUTOMATED, objective PR facts into `pr_outcomes`:\n4|    repository, branch, PR number/url, merge SHA, checks state, merge state.\n5|\n6|Explicit invariants:\n7|    - \"merged\" is NEVER recorded as ACCEPTED (that requires an explicit human/\n8|      authorized acceptance event — the service enforces it elsewhere);\n9|    - derived state mapping:\n10|        PR open                → OPEN\n11|        merged, checks green   → CI_VERIFIED\n12|        merged, checks failing → MERGED (rework signal visible, state honest)\n13|        closed unmerged        → REWORK_REQUIRED only if labeled; else OPEN→CLOSED\n14|          (we record CLOSED via rework flag only when evidence exists; unknown\n15|           data stays UNKNOWN — i.e. we leave OPEN and store raw metadata)\n16|    - unknown data remains UNKNOWN (never invented).\n17|\n18|Backfill: `backfill_recent_prs()` pulls the last N merged PRs of a repo where\n19|correlation is reliable (PR authored by the agent identity / branch naming).\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import json\n24|import os\n25|import urllib.request\n26|from dataclasses import dataclass\n27|\n28|from sqlalchemy import select\n29|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n30|\n31|from .economics_models import (\n32|    OUTCOME_CI_VERIFIED,\n33|    OUTCOME_MERGED,\n34|    OUTCOME_OPEN,\n35|    PrOutcomeRecord,\n36|)\n37|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n38|\n39|\n40|@dataclass\n41|class GithubPrFacts:\n42|    repository: str\n43|    number: int\n44|    branch: str\n45|    state: str                 # open | closed\n46|    merged: bool\n47|    merge_sha: str | None\n48|    checks_state: str | None   # success | failure | pending | None(unknown)\n49|    pr_url: str\n50|    author: str | None\n51|\n52|\n53|def fetch_repo_prs(repo: str, *, limit: int = 30, token_env: str = \"GITHUB_TOKEN\") -> list[GithubPrFacts]:\n54|    \"\"\"Read-only GitHub REST pull of recent PRs. Requires GITHUB_TOKEN env.\n55|\n56|    Never fabricates data: on API failure raises; unknown fields stay None.\n57|    \"\"\"\n58|    token = os.environ.get(token_env)\n59|    if not token:\n60|        raise RuntimeError(f\"no {token_env} configured — refusing to fabricate PR facts\")\n61|    req = urllib.request.Request(\n62|        f\"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/pulls?state=all&per_page={limit}&sort=updated&direction=desc\",\n63|        headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {token}\", \"Accept\": \"application/vnd.github+json\"},\n64|    )\n65|    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as resp:\n66|        data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n67|    out = []\n68|    for pr in data:\n69|        out.append(GithubPrFacts(\n70|            repository=repo,\n71|            number=pr[\"number\"],\n72|            branch=pr[\"head\"][\"ref\"],\n73|            state=pr[\"state\"],\n74|            merged=bool(pr.get(\"merged_at\")),\n75|            merge_sha=pr.get(\"merge_commit_sha\"),\n76|            checks_state=None,  # filled by fetch_pr_checks (separate call)\n77|            pr_url=pr[\"html_url\"],\n78|            author=(pr.get(\"user\") or {}).get(\"login\"),\n79|        ))\n80|    return out\n81|\n82|\n83|def fetch_pr_checks(repo: str, number: int, *, token_env: str = \"GITHUB_TOKEN\") -> str | None:\n84|    \"\"\"Combined check-run state for a PR's head SHA: success/failure/pending.\"\"\"\n85|    token = os.environ.get(token_env)\n86|    if not token:\n87|        return None\n88|    try:\n89|        req = urllib.request.Request(\n90|            f\"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/pulls/{number}\",\n91|            headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {token}\", \"Accept\": \"application/vnd.github+json\"},\n92|        )\n93|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as resp:\n94|            pr = json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n95|        sha = pr[\"head\"][\"sha\"]\n96|        req2 = urllib.request.Request(\n97|            f\"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/commits/{sha}/check-runs\",\n98|            headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {token}\", \"Accept\": \"application/vnd.github+json\"},\n99|        )\n100|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req2, timeout=20) as resp2:\n101|            runs = json.loads(resp2.read().decode()).get(\"check_runs\", [])\n102|        if not runs:\n103|            return None\n104|        if any(r[\"conclusion\"] == \"failure\" for r in runs):\n105|            return \"failure\"\n106|        if any(r[\"status\"] != \"completed\" for r in runs):\n107|            return \"pending\"\n108|        return \"success\"\n109|    except Exception:\n110|        return None  # unknown stays unknown\n111|\n112|\n113|def derive_outcome_state(facts: GithubPrFacts) -> str:\n114|    \"\"\"Objective state mapping — MERGED never becomes ACCEPTED here.\"\"\"\n115|    if facts.merged:\n116|        if facts.checks_state == \"success\":\n117|            return OUTCOME_CI_VERIFIED\n118|        return OUTCOME_MERGED\n119|    return OUTCOME_OPEN\n120|\n121|", "total_lines": 166, "file_size": 6600, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=121 to continue reading (showing 1-120 of 166 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

with outcome_state=ACCEPTED\n5|  cost_per_accepted_requirement = total cost / distinct accepted requirement refs\n6|\n7|Honesty rules carried over from REQ-054/058:\n8|  - ACCEPTED counts only explicit acceptance (accepted_at set); MERGED alone\n9|    never becomes accepted.\n10|  - Unknown human time stays unknown (never 0).\n11|  - Unknown cost stays unknown: entries with null api_cost_usd are counted\n12|    separately, not coerced to zero.\n13|\"\"\"\n14|from __future__ import annotations\n15|\n16|import json\n17|from datetime import date, datetime, timezone\n18|from typing import Any\n19|\n20|from sqlalchemy import select\n21|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n22|\n23|from .economics_models import (\n24|    OUTCOME_ACCEPTED,\n25|    CostAttributionRecord,\n26|    PrOutcomeRecord,\n27|    RequirementLinkRecord,\n28|)\n29|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n30|\n31|\n32|def _now() -> datetime:\n33|    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n34|\n35|\n36|class EconomicsReport:\n37|    \"\"\"Model/provider-level rollup of the canonical metrics.\"\"\"\n38|\n39|    def __init__(self) -> None:\n40|        self.total_cost_usd = 0.0\n41|        self.entries_with_unknown_cost = 0\n42|        self.failed_run_cost_usd = 0.0\n43|        self.total_tokens = 0\n44|        self.outcomes_total = 0\n45|        self.outcomes_accepted = 0\n46|        self.outcomes_merged = 0\n47|        self.outcomes_reverted = 0\n48|        self.outcomes_rework = 0\n49|        self.accepted_requirements: set[str] = set()\n50|        self.linked_requirements: set[str] = set()\n51|        self.human_review_minutes = 0.0\n52|        self.human_repair_minutes = 0.0\n53|        self.human_time_unknown_count = 0\n54|        self.first_pass_count = 0\n55|        self.first_pass_known = 0\n56|\n57|    # ------------------------------------------------------------- math\n58|    @property\n59|    def cost_per_accepted_pr(self) -> float | None:\n60|        if self.outcomes_accepted == 0:\n61|            return None\n62|        return self.total_cost_usd / self.outcomes_accepted\n63|\n64|    @property\n65|    def cost_per_accepted_requirement(self) -> float | None:\n66|        if not self.accepted_requirements:\n67|            return None\n68|        return self.total_cost_usd / len(self.accepted_requirements)\n69|\n70|    @property\n71|    def first_pass_acceptance_rate(self) -> float | None:\n72|        if self.first_pass_known == 0:\n73|            return None\n74|        return self.first_pass_count / self.first_pass_known\n75|\n76|    def to_dict(self, **filters: Any) -> dict:\n77|        return {\n78|            \"filters\": filters or None,\n79|            \"total_cost_usd\": round(self.total_cost_usd, 6),\n80|            \"entries_with_unknown_cost\": self.entries_with_unknown_cost,\n81|            \"failed_run_cost_usd\": round(self.failed_run_cost_usd, 6),\n82|            \"total_tokens\": self.total_tokens,\n83|            \"outcomes\": {\n84|                \"total\": self.outcomes_total,\n85|                \"accepted\": self.outcomes_accepted,\n86|                \"merged\": self.outcomes_merged,\n87|                \"reverted\": self.outcomes_reverted,\n88|                \"rework_required\": self.outcomes_rework,\n89|            },\n90|            \"requirements\": {\n91|                \"linked_distinct\": len(self.linked_requirements),\n92|                \"accepted_distinct\": len(self.accepted_requirements),\n93|            },\n94|            \"cost_per_accepted_pr\": (round(self.cost_per_accepted_pr, 6)\n95|                                     if self.cost_per_accepted_pr is not None else None),\n96|            \"cost_per_accepted_requirement\": (\n97|                round(self.cost_per_accepted_requirement, 6)\n98|                if self.cost_per_accepted_requirement is not None else None),\n99|            \"first_pass_acceptance_rate\": (round(self.first_pass_acceptance_rate, 4)\n100|                                           if self.first_pass_acceptance_rate is not None else None),\n101|            \"human_review_minutes\": (self.human_review_minutes or None),\n102|            \"human_repair_minutes\": (self.human_repair_minutes or None),\n103|            \"human_time_unknown_count\": self.human_time_unknown_count,\n104|        }\n105|\n106|\n107|async def build_report(db: AsyncSession, *, model_id: str | None = None,\n108|                       provider: str | None = None, repository: str | None = None,\n109|                       task_category: str | None = None,\n110|                       work_item_id: str | None = None,\n111|                       date_from: date | None = None,\n112|                       date_to: date | None = None) -> EconomicsReport:\n113|    \"\"\"Aggregate the canonical metrics with optional filters (REV2 §9B.6).\"\"\"\n114|    outcomes = (await db.execute(select(PrOutcomeRecord))).scalars().all()\n115|    costs = (await db.execute(select(CostAttributionRecord))).scalars().all()\n116|    links = (await db.execute(select(RequirementLinkRecord))).scalars().all()\n117|\n118|    by_outcome: dict[str, list[CostAttributionRecord]] = {}\n119|    for c in costs:\n120|        by_outcome.setdefault(c.outcome_id, []).append(c)\n121|\n122|    def _match(o: PrOutcomeRecord) -> bool:\n123|        if model_id and o.model_id != model_id:\n124|            return False\n125|        if provider and o.provider != provider:\n126|            return False\n127|        if repository and o.repository != repository:\n128|            return False\n129|        if task_category and o.task_category != task_category:\n130|            return False\n131|        if work_item_id and o.work_item_id != work_item_id:\n132|            return False\n133|        if date_from and o.created_at and o.created_at.date() < date_from:\n134|            return False\n135|        if date_to and o.created_at and o.created_at.date() > date_to:\n136|            return False\n137|        return True\n138|\n139|    matched = {o.outcome_id: o for o in outcomes if _match(o)}\n140|    links_by_outcome: dict[str, list[RequirementLinkRecord]] = {}\n141|    for l in links:\n142|        links_by_outcome.setdefault(l.outcome_id, []).append(l)\n143|\n144|    rep = EconomicsReport()\n145|    for oid, o in matched.items():\n146|        rep.outcomes_total += 1\n147|        if o.outcome_state == OUTCOME_ACCEPTED:\n148|            rep.outcomes_accepted += 1\n149|            for l in links_by_outcome.get(oid, []):\n150|                rep.accepted_requirements.add(l.requirement_ref)\n151|        elif o.outcome_state == \"MERGED\":\n152|            rep.outcomes_merged += 1\n153|        elif o.outcome_state == \"REVERTED\":\n154|            rep.outcomes_reverted += 1\n155|        elif o.outcome_state == \"REWORK_REQUIRED\":\n156|            rep.outcomes_rework += 1\n157|        for l in links_by_outcome.get(oid, []):\n158|            rep.linked_requirements.add(l.requirement_ref)\n159|        if o.human_review_minutes is None and o.human_repair_minutes is None:\n160|            rep.human_time_unknown_count += 1\n161|        else:\n162|            rep.human_review_minutes += o.human_review_minutes or 0.0\n163|            rep.human_repair_minutes += o.human_repair_minutes or 0.0\n164|        if o.first_pass is not None:\n165|            rep.first_pass_known += 1\n166|            rep.first_pass_count += 1 if o.first_pass else 0\n167|        for c in by_outcome.get(oid, []):\n168|            if c.api_cost_usd is None:\n169|                rep.entries_with_unknown_cost += 1\n170|            else:\n171|                rep.total_cost_usd += c.api_cost_usd\n172|                if c.failed_run:\n173|                    rep.failed_run_cost_usd += c.api_cost_usd\n174|            toks = (c.input_tokens or 0) + (c.output_tokens or 0)\n175|            rep.total_tokens += toks\n176|\n177|    return rep\n178|\n179|\n180|async def link_requirements(db: AsyncSession, outcome_id: str,\n181|                            requirement_refs: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n182|    \"\"\"Idempotent many-to-many link of one PR outcome to requirement refs.\"\"\"\n183|    rows = (await db.execute(\n184|        select(RequirementLinkRecord).where(RequirementLinkRecord.outcome_id == outcome_id)\n185|    )).scalars().all()\n186|    have = {r.requirement_ref for r in rows}\n187|    added = []\n188|    for ref in requirement_refs:\n189|        ref = ref.strip()\n190|        if ref and ref not in have:\n191|            db.add(RequirementLinkRecord(link_id=RequirementLinkRecord.new_id(),\n192|                                         outcome_id=outcome_id, requirement_ref=ref))\n193|            added.append(ref)\n194|    await db.commit()\n195|    return added\n196|\n197|\n198|async def attach_session_cost(db: AsyncSession, outcome_id: str,\n199|                              session_id: str) -> dict:\n200|    \"\"\"Create a cost-attribution entry from an execution session's telemetry\n201|    (source=session). Missing fields stay null — never zero-filled.\"\"\"\n202|    from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord\n203|\n204|    s = await db.get(ExecutionSessionRecord, session_id)\n205|    if s is None:\n206|        raise ValueError(\"session not found\")\n207|    entry = CostAttributionRecord(\n208|        entry_id=CostAttributionRecord.new_id(), outcome_id=outcome_id,\n209|        session_id=session_id, model_id=s.model_id, provider=s.provider,\n210|        api_cost_usd=s.estimated_cost_usd,  # blended authoritative estimate\n211|        source=\"session\", failed_run=False,\n212|    )\n213|    db.add(entry)\n214|    await db.commit()\n215|    return {\"entry_id\": entry.entry_id, \"api_cost_usd\": entry.api_cost_usd}\n216|\n217|\n218|async def set_outcome_state(db: AsyncSession, outcome: PrOutcomeRecord, *,\n219|                            new_state: str, actor: str = \"api\",\n220|                            accepted_by: str | None = None) -> None:\n221|    \"\"\"Transition outcome state. ACCEPTED requires an explicit accepted_by —\n222|    the human/product acceptance event (never inferred from MERGED).\"\"\"\n223|    from .economics_models import OUTCOME_STATES\n224|\n225|    if new_state not in OUTCOME_STATES:\n226|        raise ValueError(f\"invalid outcome state: {new_state}\")\n227|    if new_state == OUTCOME_ACCEPTED and not accepted_by:\n228|        raise ValueError(\"ACCEPTED requires accepted_by (explicit human acceptance)\")\n229|    prev = outcome.outcome_state\n230|    outcome.outcome_state = new_state\n231|    if new_state == OUTCOME_ACCEPTED:\n232|        outcome.accepted_at = _now()\n233|        outcome.accepted_by = accepted_by\n234|    await add_event(db, event_type=\"PR_OUTCOME_STATE\", actor_source=\"economics_service\",\n235|                    agent_id=outcome.agent_id or \"\",\n236|                    summary=f\"Outcome {outcome.repository or ''}#{outcome.pr_number or outcome.outcome_id[:8]} \"\n237|                            f\"{prev} -> {new_state}\",\n238|                    metadata={\"outcome_id\": outcome.outcome_id, \"prior_state\": prev,\n239|                              \"new_state\": new_state, \"actor\": actor})\n240|    await db.commit()\n241|\n242|\n243|def commits_json(shas: list[str]) -> str:\n244|    return json.dumps(shas)\n245|\n246|\n247|def commits_list(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:\n248|    return json.loads(raw) if raw else []\n249|", "total_lines": 248, "file_size": 10356, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"A2A work dispatch — the single authoritative path for starting NEW cloud\n2|execution of a Work Item on a worker agent (REV2 Phase C, REQ-059 gate).\n3|\n4|Design rules from the plan:\n5|- ONE dispatch point. UI/API layers call this service; budget checks are never\n6|  duplicated in several callers.\n7|- Gate BEFORE new cloud execution: budget execution-check verdict decides.\n8|  - below hard limit  → dispatch\n9|  - soft exceeded     → dispatch + retain the warning in the event trail\n10|  - hard exceeded     → no NEW dispatch; durable event + Attention item\n11|                        (EXECUTION_REJECTED); in-flight work untouched\n12|  - hard + override   → dispatch + audited override trail already exists\n13|- Unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never a fabricated zero (the budget engine\n14|  itself never blocks on unknown cost; the dispatch path preserves the fact).\n15|- Idempotency: a client-supplied idempotency key (or a stable hash of the\n16|  work/assignment pair) maps to ONE execution task; retries return the\n17|  original task instead of dispatching twice.\n18|- Correlation recorded on the task + event: Work ID/key, assignment, bridge\n19|  run id, budget-check verdict (audit trail), external A2A task id.\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import hashlib\n24|import json\n25|from typing import Any\n26|\n27|from sqlalchemy import select\n28|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n29|\n30|from . import budget_service, work_service\n31|from .session_lifecycle import fail_session_for_dispatch, open_session_for_dispatch\n32|from .bridge import BridgeError, get_bridge_for_agent\n33|from .telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n34|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n35|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskCreate, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n36|\n37|\n38|class DispatchError(Exception):\n39|    \"\"\"Machine-readable dispatch failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n40|\n41|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n42|        super().__init__(code)\n43|        self.code = code\n44|        self.http_status = http_status\n45|        self.detail = detail or {}\n46|\n47|\n48|def _idempotency_key(work_item_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n49|                     client_key: str | None) -> str:\n50|    \"\"\"Stable dedupe key. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n51|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n52|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n53|\n54|\n55|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n56|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n57|\n58|    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n59|    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n60|    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer double as the idem-\n61|    potency ledger. The event log is the same durability class ACMS already\n62|    relies on for reassignment counting (PR #32 pattern).\n63|    \"\"\"\n64|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n65|        select(AgentEventRecord)\n66|        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type == \"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\")\n67|        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.timestamp.desc())\n68|        .limit(200)\n69|    )).all()\n70|    for ev in rows:  # newest first\n71|        try:\n72|            meta = json.loads(ev.metadata_json) if ev.metadata_json else {}\n73|        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n74|            continue\n75|        if meta.get(\"dedupe_key\") == dedupe_key and meta.get(\"task_id\"):\n76|            return await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, meta[\"task_id\"])\n77|    return None\n78|\n79|\n80|async def dispatch_work(db: AsyncSession, *, work_item_id: str,\n81|                        instruction: str, agent_id: str | None = None,\n82|                        idempotency_key: str | None = None,\n83|                        session_id: str | None = None,\n84|                        actor: str = \"api\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n85|    \"\"\"Dispatch NEW cloud execution for a Work Item — the ONLY path that does.\n86|\n87|    Returns a dict:\n88|      dispatched: bool          — False when the gate blocked or dedupe hit\n89|      reason: str               — dispatched | hard_budget_exceeded | duplicate\n90|      task_id / external_task_id — correlation ids\n91|      budget_check: dict        — the verdict used for the gate (audit trail)\n92|    Raises DispatchError for structural failures (unknown work/agent/bridge).\n93|    \"\"\"\n94|    # ---- 1. resolve the work item (+ open assignment) ----------------------\n95|    work = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n96|    if work is None:\n97|        raise DispatchError(\"unknown-work-item\", 404)\n98|    assignments = await work_service.list_assignments(db, work_item_id=work_item_id)\n99|    active = next((a for a in assignments if a.status == \"ACTIVE\"), None)\n100|    if agent_id is None and active is None:\n101|        raise DispatchError(\"no-active-assignment\", 409)\n102|    target_agent = agent_id or active.agent_id\n103|    if active is not None and agent_id is not None and agent_id != active.agent_id:\n104|        raise DispatchError(\"agent-mismatch\", 409,\n105|                            {\"active_assignment_agent\": active.agent_id})\n106|\n107|    # ---- 2. idempotency: a retry must never double-dispatch ----------------\n108|    dedupe_key = _idempotency_key(work_item_id, active.assignment_id if active else None,\n109|                                  idempotency_key)\n110|    existing = await _find_existing_task(db, dedupe_key)\n111|    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"RUNNING\":\n112|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"duplicate\",\n113|                \"task_id\": existing.task_id, \"external_task_id\": None,\n114|                \"budget_check\": None}\n115|\n116|    # ---- 3. budget gate (REQ-059) BEFORE any cloud execution ---------------\n117|    verdict = await budget_service.check_new_execution_allowed(db, work_item_id)\n118|    if not verdict.get(\"allowed\", True):\n119|        # hard exceeded without override → durable rejection event (Attention\n120|        # picks it up from the log; no separate mutable state) + hold dispatch.\n121|        await add_event(\n122|            db,\n123|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n124|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n125|            agent_id=target_agent,\n126|            work_key=work.work_key,\n127|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n128|            summary=(\"NEW cloud execution blocked: hard budget exceeded on \"\n129|                     f\"{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]} \"\n130|                     f\"(state={verdict.get('budget_state')}; \"\n131|                     f\"override={'active' if (verdict.get('override') or {}).get('active') else 'none'})\")[:512],\n132|            metadata={\"budget_check\": verdict, \"dispatch_attempt\": True,\n133|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key},\n134|        )\n135|        await db.commit()\n136|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"hard_budget_exceeded\",\n137|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None,\n138|                \"budget_check\": verdict}\n139|\n140|    # ---- 4. resolve the bridge target --------------------------------------\n141|    bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(target_agent)\n142|    if bridge is None:\n143|        raise DispatchError(\"no-bridge-target\", 503, {\"agent_id\": target_agent})\n144|\n145|    # ---- 4b. pre-create the execution task so the runtime can call back ----\n146|    # ADR-0012: the completion callback binds task_id + agent id, so the task\n147|    # row must exist BEFORE the run starts and its id must travel with the\n148|    # instruction. Failure to record the task aborts the dispatch (nothing\n149|    # was sent to the bridge — budget not consumed, no session created).\n150|    task, err = await work_service.create_execution_task(db, ExecutionTaskCreate(\n151|        work_item_id=work_item_id,\n152|        agent_id=target_agent,\n153|        external_task_id=None,  # a2a run id back-filled after send_work\n154|    ))\n155|    if task is None:\n156|        raise DispatchError(err or \"task-record-failed\", 500)\n157|\n158|    # ADR-0012 primary completion path: the instruction carries the callback\n159|    # contract so the runtime reports its terminal result itself. The\n160|    # reconcile sweep stays the fallback for lost/late callbacks.\n161|    from .settings import get_settings\n162|\n163|    callback_base = get_settings().callback_base_url or \"\"\n164|    dispatch_instruction = instruction\n165|    if callback_base:\n166|        dispatch_instruction = (\n167|            f\"{instruction}\\n\\n---\\nCOMPLETION PROTOCOL (ACMS ADR-0012): when this task \"\n168|            f\"reaches a terminal state, POST to \"\n169|            f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion with header \"\n170|            f\"\\\"Authorization: Bearer <ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN>\\\" and JSON body \"\n171|            f'{{\"task_id\": \"{task.task_id}\", \"acms_agent_id\": \"{target_agent}\", '\n172|            f'\"status\": \"SUCCEEDED\"|\"FAILED\"|\"CANCELLED\", \"completed_at\": \"<UTC ISO>\", '\n173|            f'\"result_reference\": \"<PR/artifact URL>\", \"error_summary\": \"<one line when failed>\"}}. '\n174|            f\"Your agent id for acms_agent_id is {target_agent}. This is mandatory; \"\n175|            f\"ACMS reconciliation only repairs lost callbacks.\"\n176|        )\n177|\n178|    # ---- 5. dispatch through the bridge (new A2A run) ----------------------\n179|    session_for_run = None  # set on success; failure path closes it if set\n180|    try:\n181|        result = bridge.send_work(\n182|            work_key=work.work_key or work_item_id,\n183|            assignment_key=(active.assignment_key if active else \"\") or \"\",\n184|            instruction=dispatch_instruction,\n185|            session_id=session_id,\n186|        )\n187|    except BridgeError as e:\n188|        # dispatch itself failed — record it; budget NOT consumed (ACMS did not\n189|        # create a session), retry after the error is resolved.\n190|        await add_event(\n191|            db,\n192|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n193|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n194|            agent_id=target_agent,\n195|            work_key=work.work_key,\n196|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n197|            summary=f\"A2A dispatch failed: {str(e)[:180]}\",\n198|            metadata={\"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n199|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id},\n200|        )\n201|        await fail_session_for_dispatch(db, session=session_for_run, reason=str(e))\n202|        await db.commit()\n203|        raise DispatchError(\"bridge-error\", 502, {\"detail\": str(e)[:300]}) from None\n204|\n205|    run_id = (result or {}).get(\"run_id\") or (result or {}).get(\"id\")\n206|\n207|    # back-fill the A2A run id on the pre-created task (ADR-0012 binding)\n208|    if run_id:\n209|        task.external_task_id = str(run_id)\n210|\n211|    # Phase F (plan §8): the dispatch automatically owns an ExecutionSession.\n212|    # Idempotent on the A2A run id — a duplicate dispatch never duplicates it.\n213|    session_for_run = await open_session_for_dispatch(\n214|        db, agent_id=str(target_agent), work_item_id=work_item_id,\n215|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n216|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n217|        harness_session_id=(result or {}).get(\"session_id\"),\n218|    )\n219|\n220|    # ---- 6. execution task pre-created in step 4b (ADR-0012); the dedupe\n221|    # ledger is preserved on the event metadata (``disp-<key>`` remains the\n222|    # client-visible idempotency marker) ------------------------\n223|\n224|    # ---- 7. durable audit trail --------------------------------------------\n225|    await add_event(\n226|        db,\n227|        event_type=\"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\",\n228|        actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n229|        agent_id=target_agent,\n230|        work_key=work.work_key,\n231|        assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n232|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n233|        summary=(f\"Dispatched [{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}] to {str(target_agent)[:8]}\"\n234|                 + (f\" (soft exceeded: {verdict.get('budget_state')})\"\n235|                    if verdict.get(\"budget_state\") == \"SOFT_EXCEEDED\" else \"\"))[:512],\n236|        metadata={\"run_id\": run_id, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n237|                  \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n238|                  \"assignment_id\": active.assignment_id if active else None,\n239|                  \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"actor\": actor},\n240|    )\n241|    await db.commit()\n242|    return {\"dispatched\": True, \"reason\": verdict.get(\"reason\", \"dispatched\"),\n243|            \"task_id\": task.task_id, \"external_task_id\": str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n244|            \"budget_check\": verdict}\n245|", "total_lines": 244, "file_size": 12122, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
