{"content": "1|\"\"\"Background scheduling for live telemetry (combined slice 3+4; ADR-0010; plan §16).\n2|\n3|Lightweight in-process loop — no Celery/K8s/event bus. Runs inside the FastAPI\n4|lifespan; every DB operation gets its own session so the loop never holds a\n5|request-scoped session. Time is injected (``now_fn``) so tests never wait real\n6|minutes/hours (plan §20).\n7|\n8|Jobs:\n9|- stale evaluation (every tick): derive UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE\n10|- 1-hour stale reconciliation: actively request a full status snapshot\n11|- 24 h fleet reconciliation (staggered tolerance documented in ADR-0010)\n12|- telemetry sampling every ACMS_TELEMETRY_SAMPLE_SECONDS + on material change\n13|\n14|Reconciliation authority (plan §9): correct operational telemetry, preserve\n15|history, log discrepancy events; never touch approved scope/assignment.\n16|Multi-replica note: a single ACMS instance is assumed; replicas would need\n17|leader/lock coordination (documented in ADR-0010 + FUTURE_WORK).\n18|\"\"\"\n19|from __future__ import annotations\n20|\n21|import asyncio\n22|import json\n23|import logging\n24|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n25|\n26|from sqlalchemy import select\n27|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n28|\n29|from .db import SessionLocal\n30|from .telemetry_models import (\n31|    AgentEventRecord,\n32|    AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n33|    AgentTelemetrySampleRecord,\n34|    CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY,\n35|    CONNECTIVITY_STALE,\n36|    CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE,\n37|    CONTACT_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION,\n38|)\n39|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService, _new_id, add_event, _now\n40|from .settings import get_settings\n41|\n42|logger = logging.getLogger(\"acms.telemetry.scheduler\")\n43|\n44|\n45|class TelemetryScheduler:\n46|    def __init__(self, session_factory=None, now_fn=None):\n47|        self._session_factory = session_factory or SessionLocal\n48|        self._now_fn = now_fn or _now\n49|        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None\n50|        self._last_fleet_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n51|        self._last_result_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n52|        self._last_sample: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n53|        # C4 state-entry dedupe: AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT is emitted once\n54|        # per state entry (never per tick). Key = agent_id, value = currently in state.\n55|        self._running_unassigned: dict[str, bool] = {}\n56|\n57|    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ loop\n58|\n59|    def start(self) -> None:\n60|        if self._task is None or self._task.done():\n61|            self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._run(), name=\"acms-telemetry-scheduler\")\n62|    async def stop(self) -> None:\n63|        if self._task is not None:\n64|            self._task.cancel()\n65|            try:\n66|                await self._task\n67|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n68|                pass\n69|            self._task = None\n70|\n71|    async def _run(self) -> None:\n72|        from .settings import get_settings\n73|\n74|        while True:\n75|            try:\n76|                s = get_settings()\n77|                await self.tick(s)\n78|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n79|                raise\n80|            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — scheduler must survive transient DB issues\n81|                logger.exception(\"telemetry scheduler tick failed\")\n82|            await asyncio.sleep(5)\n83|    async def tick(self, s) -> None:\n84|        \"\"\"One scheduler pass. Public so tests can drive time deterministically.\"\"\"\n85|        now_dt = self._now_fn()\n86|        async with self._session_factory() as db:\n87|            statuses = await TelemetryService.list_status(db)\n88|            for status in statuses:\n89|                await self._evaluate_agent(db, s, status, now_dt)\n90|            await self._maybe_fleet_reconcile(db, s, now_dt)\n91|            await self._reconcile_execution_results(db, s, now_dt)\n92|\n93|    # ------------------------------------------------------- per-agent logic\n94|\n95|    async def _evaluate_agent(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n96|        agent_id = status.agent_id\n97|        if status.last_contact_at is None:\n98|            return  # never contacted: stays UNKNOWN\n99|\n100|        stale_after = timedelta(seconds=s.heartbeat_stale_seconds)\n101|        reconcile_after = timedelta(seconds=s.stale_reconcile_seconds)\n102|        lc = status.last_contact_at\n103|        if lc.tzinfo is None:\n104|            from datetime import timezone as _tz\n105|            lc = lc.replace(tzinfo=_tz.utc)\n106|        delta = now_dt - lc\n107|\n108|        new_connectivity = status.connectivity\n109|        if delta <= stale_after:\n110|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY\n111|        elif delta >= reconcile_after:\n112|            ok = await self._attempt_reconciliation(db, s, status, now_dt)\n113|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY if ok else CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE\n114|        else:\n115|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_STALE\n116|\n117|        if new_connectivity != status.connectivity:\n118|            prev = status.connectivity\n119|            status.connectivity = new_connectivity\n120|            if new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_STALE and status.stale_since is None:\n121|                status.stale_since = now_dt\n122|            elif new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY:\n123|                status.stale_since = None\n124|            status.updated_at = now_dt\n125|            await add_event(\n126|                db,\n127|                event_type=\"CONNECTIVITY_CHANGED\", actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=agent_id,\n128|                summary=f\"Connectivity {prev} -> {new_connectivity} after {int(delta.total_seconds())}s without contact\",\n129|            )\n130|        await db.commit()\n131|\n132|        # C4: RUNNING without a primary ACMS assignment = informational state.\n133|        # Human direction 2026-09-29: do NOT automatically stop the runtime.\n134|        # Emit once per state entry; auto-remediation is deliberately absent.\n135|        await self._check_running_unassigned(db, status, now_dt)\n136|\n137|        # periodic telemetry sample (plan §8)\n138|        last = self._last_sample.get(agent_id)\n139|        if last is None or (now_dt - last).total_seconds() >= s.telemetry_sample_seconds:\n140|            await self._write_sample(db, status, now_dt)\n141|            self._last_sample[agent_id] = now_dt\n142|\n143|    async def _check_running_unassigned(self, db: AsyncSession, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n144|                                         now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n145|        \"\"\"Informational warning when the harness reports the agent RUNNING but\n146|        ACMS holds no ACTIVE primary assignment (Phase C4). No auto-remediation:\n147|        the runtime keeps running; humans see the Attention item.\"\"\"\n148|        from sqlalchemy import select\n149|        from .work_models import AssignmentRecord\n150|\n151|        running = (status.agent_running or \"\").lower() == \"true\"\n152|        in_state = self._running_unassigned.get(status.agent_id, False)\n153|        if not running:\n154|            if in_state:  # leaving the state: reset dedupe so a future entry re-emits\n155|                self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = False\n156|            return\n157|        active = (await db.execute(\n158|            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n159|                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == status.agent_id,\n160|                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n161|            ).limit(1)\n162|        )).scalars().first()\n163|        if active is not None:\n164|            if in_state:\n165|                self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = False\n166|            return\n167|        if in_state:\n168|            return  # already announced this entry — never per-tick spam\n169|        self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = True\n170|        await add_event(\n171|            db,\n172|            event_type=\"AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT\",\n173|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n174|            summary=(\"Agent RUNNING with no active primary ACMS assignment — \"\n175|                     \"informational; no automatic remediation\"),\n176|            metadata={\"detection\": \"scheduler\", \"policy\": \"observe-only\"},\n177|        )\n178|        await db.commit()\n179|\n180|    async def _attempt_reconciliation(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> bool:\n181|        \"\"\"Request a full status snapshot from the bridge (1 h stale path).\n182|\n183|        Bridge transport lands in Phase 4; until then reconciliation of an\n184|        agent without a live bridge fails (→ UNREACHABLE), which is the honest\n185|        outcome: we cannot reach it.\"\"\"\n186|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_STARTED\",\n187|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n188|            summary=f\"Stale >= {s.stale_reconcile_seconds}s - requesting status snapshot\")\n189|        await db.commit()\n190|        try:\n191|            from .bridge import get_bridge_for_agent  # Phase 4 module\n192|\n193|            bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(status.agent_id)\n194|            snapshot = await bridge.fetch_status()\n195|            await TelemetryService.ingest_heartbeat(db, status.agent_id, snapshot)\n196|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_SUCCEEDED\",\n197|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n198|                summary=\"Reconciliation snapshot ingested; connectivity restored\")\n199|            await db.commit()\n200|            return True\n201|        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — no bridge / transport failure = unreachable\n202|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_FAILED\",\n203|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n204|                summary=f\"Reconciliation failed: {type(exc).__name__}\",\n205|                metadata={\"error\": str(exc)[:300]})\n206|            await db.commit()\n207|            return False\n208|\n209|    async def _reconcile_execution_results(self, db: AsyncSession, s, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n210|        \"\"\"ADR-0012 fallback: close nonterminal ACMS tasks/sessions whose A2A\n211|        run is terminal on the bridge (lost callback / partition / restart /\n212|        late callback repair path). Primary signal remains the callback.\n213|\n214|        Rate-limited: sweeps at most every ``ACMS_CALLBACK_RECONCILE_SECONDS``\n215|        (default 300s) so a tick loop never hammers the bridge.\n216|        \"\"\"\n217|        interval = max(60, int(getattr(s, \"callback_reconcile_seconds\", 300)))\n218|        last = self._last_result_reconcile\n219|        if last is not None and (now_dt - last).total_seconds() < interval:\n220|            return\n221|        self._last_result_reconcile = now_dt\n222|\n223|        from . import bridge as _bridge_mod\n224|\n225|        get_bridge_for_agent = _bridge_mod.get_bridge_for_agent\n226|        from .work_models import ExecutionTaskRecord\n227|\n228|        nonterminal = (await db.execute(\n229|            select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n230|            .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.status == \"RUNNING\")\n231|            .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.external_task_id.isnot(None))\n232|            .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.external_task_id.notlike(\"disp-%\"))\n233|            .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.asc())\n234|            .limit(20)\n235|        )).scalars().all()\n236|        repaired = 0  # empty task batch still falls through to the orphan-session sweep\n237|        for task in nonterminal:\n238|            if not task.agent_id:\n239|                continue  # unassigned task rows are never bridge-reconciled\n240|            try:\n241|                bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(task.agent_id)\n242|                run = bridge._get(f\"/v1/runs/{task.external_task_id}\")\n243|            except Exception:  # ADR-0012 §C5: reconciliation repairs SESSION state too — close the\n255|            # OPEN ExecutionSession correlated to this A2A run (fail-safe: never\n256|            # reopen or fabricate; only OPEN sessions for the same run id).\n257|            from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord as _ESR\n258|\n259|            sess = (await db.scalars(\n260|                select(_ESR)\n261|                .where(_ESR.a2a_task_id == task.external_task_id)\n262|                .where(_ESR.status == \"OPEN\")\n263|                .order_by(_ESR.started_at.desc())\n264|                .limit(1)\n265|            )).first()\n266|            session_id = None\n267|            if sess is not None:\n268|                sess.status = \"CLOSED\"\n269|                sess.ended_at = now_dt\n270|                session_id = sess.session_id\n271|            repaired += 1\n272|            await add_event(\n273|                db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_RECONCILED\", actor_source=\"result_reconciliation\",\n274|                agent_id=task.agent_id, work_key=None,\n275|                a2a_task_id=task.external_task_id,\n276|                summary=(f\"Reconciler closed task {task.task_id[:12]} → {mapped} \"\n277|                         f\"from bridge run status '{run_status}' (callback lost/late)\"),\n278|                metadata={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"a2a_run_id\": task.external_task_id,\n279|                          \"session_id\": session_id, \"source\": \"adr0012_reconciliation\"},\n280|            )\n281|        if repaired:\n282|            await db.commit()\n283|\n284|        # A runtime cannot\n16|close another runtime's task. Unknown task ids are REJECTED with an audit\n17|event; wrong agent identity fails CLOSED with an audit event.\n18|\n19|Idempotency/authority:\n20|    duplicate callback        → harmless (idempotent success, no dup close)\n21|    late callback after close → harmless (idempotent)\n22|    unknown task id           → 404 + audit event\n23|    wrong agent/runtime       → 403 fail-closed + audit {}\n50|\n51|\n52|class CompletionCallbackRequest(BaseModel):\n53|    \"\"\"Durable semantic payload only — never transcripts or token streams.\"\"\"\n54|\n55|    task_id: str = Field(max_length=64)\n56|    acms_agent_id: str = Field(max_length=36)\n57|    a2a_run_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=255)\n58|    status: str = Field(max_length=16)\n59| already_terminal} — both are\n70|    idempotent successes. {"output": "async def assign_primary(\n    db: AsyncSession, payload: AssignmentCreate, assigned_by: str | None = None\n) -> tuple[AssignmentRecord | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Create a primary assignment, enforcing the ACMS-REQ-008 invariant.\n\n    Returns (record, None) on success, or (None, error_code) where error_code is\n    one of: 'unknown-agent' | 'unknown-work-item' | 'work-item-not-open' |\n    'agent-has-active-assignment'.\n    \"\"\"\n    agent = await db.get(AgentRecord, payload.agent_id)\n    if agent is None:\n        return None, \"unknown-agent\"\n    work_item = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, payload.work_item_id)\n    if work_item is None:\n        return None, \"unknown-work-item\"\n    if work_item.status not in (WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value, WorkItemStatus.ACTIVE.value):\n        return None, \"work-item-not-open\"\n\n    open_assignment = (\n        await db.scalars(\n            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == payload.agent_id,\n                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n            )\n        )\n    ).first()\n    if open_assignment is not None:\n        return None, \"agent-has-active-assignment\"\n\n    record = AssignmentRecord(\nasync def set_budget(db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str, *,\n                     soft_budget_usd: float | None,\n                     hard_budget_usd: float | None,\n                     actor: str = \"api\") -> tuple[WorkBudgetRecord, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Create/update the budget row for a Work Item. Setting thresholds clears\n    any active override (documented one-shot semantics).\"\"\"\n    from .work_models import WorkItemRecord\n\n    item = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n    if item is None:\n        raise ValueError(\"work item not found\")\n\n    budget = await db.get(WorkBudgetRecord, work_item_id)\n    created = budget is None\n    if budget is None:\n        budget = WorkBudgetRecord(work_item_id=work_item_id)", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

[Subdirectory context discovered: work/llm-manager-project-framework/AGENTS.md]
# AGENTS.md — LLM Manager

These instructions apply to AI agents modifying the LLM Manager repository or MARION-IA-USA LLM Manager deployment.

## 1. Read before changing anything

Read, in order:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `FUTURE_WORK.md`
4. `SPRINT.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`
7. `docs/OPERATIONS_AND_USAGE.md`
8. relevant ADRs/TDRs

Approved requirements define scope. Future work is not authorization to implement.

## 2. Service boundary

LLM Manager owns inference/runtime infrastructure concerns. ACMS owns work/project/context orchestration.

Do not move project requirements, Kanban/task state, persistent transcript history, human steering governance, or general ACMS context management into LLM Manager merely because an integration needs them.

## 3. Production safety rules

- Treat MARION-IA-USA as production infrastructure.
- Never replace a known-working model/runtime before capturing its exact rollback configuration.
- Put deployments into explicit maintenance before authorized manual work.
- Recovery has historically had more than one trigger; verify both desired service state and desired power/VM state before stopping a VM.
- Do not assume a stopped model stays stopped until recovery state is verified.
- Never start two VMs that share the same passthrough GPU devices.
- Avoid repeated rapid GPU-VM stop/start cycles on MIAM-00111; a known PCIe/AER/riser condition has previously required a host reboot to recover.
- Preserve custom/patched vLLM environments. Do not `pip install -U vllm` into a known-good runtime.
- Experimental model stacks belong in versioned paths/containers/VMs.

## 4. Configuration and model truth

The live registry and verified runtime state are authoritative for current deployments.

Do not infer that an alias is local merely from its name. Verify provider/runtime/model identity.

When documenting a model deployment, capture:

- actual artifact/revision/checksum;
- runtime/image/commit;
- quantization;
- host/VM/site;
- context;
- TP/PP/DP/EP and relevant runtime flags;
- RAM/VRAM/hardware;
- route/alias;
- health and benchmark evidence.

## 5. History and rollback

Deployment history must make engineering work reproducible.

- `Load Configuration` is non-destructive.
- `Restore & Serve` is a separate confirmed operation.
- Failed experiments may be retained but hidden by default.
- Do not delete the last known-good revision merely because a newer model works.

## 6. Benchmark discipline

A performance number without deployment context is misleading.

Link benchmarks to exact deployment revisions and record at least:

- TTFT;
- output tokens/sec per request/agent;
- aggregate output tokens/sec;
- concurrency;
- prompt/output token counts;
- tested context;
- RAM/VRAM;
- host/hardware/runtime;
- errors;
- representative Hermes/tool-use status when relevant.

When comparing models, prefer same-node tests. When comparing nodes, use the same model/configuration first so hardware effects are visible.

## 7. Secrets and data

Never commit:

- `.env` files containing secrets;
- API keys/tokens;
- passwords;
- SSH/private keys;
- production database row dumps;
- customer-sensitive data;
- raw logs containing credentials.

Sanitize configuration into `.example` files. Run a secret scan before commit. If a secret appears in a tool transcript or repository candidate, stop and surface it for rotation/removal.

## 8. Git workflow

- Work on a dedicated branch.
- Branch format: `type/ticket-short-description`.
- Commit format: `type(scope): short description (#issue-id)`.
- Do not invent an issue ID; create/use an approved issue according to repository policy.
- Keep capture/bootstrapping changes reviewable.
- A human must approve before merge.

## 9. ADR policy

If a human explicitly made a significant architecture decision, record it as an ADR and surface it in the PR.

If the agent thinks a significant new decision is required, draft `Status: Proposed` and stop for human approval before treating it as accepted.

## 10. TDR policy

Use a TDR for intentional shortcuts, patched runtimes, manual recovery procedures, missing automation, known hardware/software limitations, or other accepted debt.

## 11. Validation

Run the exact project validation commands once captured/documented. At minimum for a Python service, consider syntax/import checks, tests, config parsing, migration/schema verification, and API smoke tests in a safe environment.

Report `Pass`, `Fail`, or `Not run` accurately. Never infer a passing test.

## 12. Stop and ask for human judgment when

- requirements conflict;
- an ACMS-facing contract would be invented or materially changed;
- a destructive production action is required outside the approved maintenance scope;
- credentials/access are required and unavailable;
- a security boundary changes;
- production tests fail unexpectedly;
- a new persistent model policy would be made without approved authority;
- the diff expands beyond the approved capture/documentation scope.

## 13. Handoff standard

At completion provide:

1. requirements addressed;
2. source/code captured and source locations;
3. files excluded and why;
4. changes made;
5. validation commands/results;
6. production impact (if any);
7. ADR/TDR status;
8. future-work items discovered;
9. known risks/unresolved questions;
10. exact branch, commit, PR, and recommended next action.
